Model before rewriting:

find x: set (size 2) of int(1..3)

such that

and([ i in x | i : int(1..2) ])

--

and([ i in x | i : int(1..2) ])
   ~~> select_representation_unconstrained ([("ReprGeneral", 9900)])
and([ i in x | i : int(1..2) ])
new variables:
  find x#packed_1: int(0..7)
new constraints:
  sum([(x#packed_1 / 1) % 2,(x#packed_1 / 2) % 2,(x#packed_1 / 4) % 2;int(1..)]) = 2

--

x#packed_1
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x#packed_1
new variables:
  find x#packed_1#rank_1: bool
  find x#packed_1#rank_2: bool
  find x#packed_1#rank_3: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(x#packed_1#rank_3),or([!(x#packed_1#rank_2),or([!(x#packed_1#rank_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

x#packed_1
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x#packed_1

--

x#packed_1
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x#packed_1
